  • No gloss, glow, drop shadow, outline, gradient, metallic or busy texture on any type, panel, rule, button or icon.
    H011 · human LookInspect the named output against this requirement and its linked references.
    No type, panel or rule on “Summit speaker card - Marguerite Halloran” (speaker-card-r002.png) carries a drop shadow, glow, outline, gloss, metallic finish, gradient or busy texture.
  • No colour outside the six palette hexes for type, grounds, panels, rules, buttons or icons, apart from the sitter ground.
    H027 · human MeasureEyedropper the centre of each flat area, rule and thick letter stroke outside the photograph; answer No if any clearly matches none of the six.
    Apart from the sitter's ground, all type, grounds, panels, rules and icons on “Summit speaker card - Marguerite Halloran” (speaker-card-r002.png) use only #23303A, #2C4A3B, #D9DDDE, #F2EEE7, #B06B3E and #1A1F24.
  • Every palette colour is used solid, at 100% opacity, with no tint or transparency.
    H027 · human MeasureEyedropper the centre of each flat area, rule and thick letter stroke outside the photograph; answer No if any clearly matches none of the six.
    Apart from the sitter's ground, all type, grounds, panels, rules and icons on “Summit speaker card - Marguerite Halloran” (speaker-card-r002.png) use only #23303A, #2C4A3B, #D9DDDE, #F2EEE7, #B06B3E and #1A1F24.
  • Every palette colour is used solid, at 100% opacity, with no tint or transparency.

  • No scrim, gradient or overlay of any colour, black and white included, is laid over a photograph.
    H028 · human LookInspect the named output against this requirement and its linked references.

  • Cascadia Slate is a mandatory colour.
    H029 · human MeasureEyedropper the dark grounds and bands outside the photograph; answer Yes if any reads at or very near #23303A.

  • Warm Paper is a mandatory colour.
    H067 · human MeasureEyedropper the light grounds and panels outside the photograph; answer Yes if any reads at or very near #F2EEE7.

  • Grounds and panels are Warm Paper or Cascadia Slate and nothing else, apart from the sitter ground.

  • No visible retouching signature.
    H063 · human LookInspect the named output against this requirement and its linked references.

  • No glossy warm filter is laid over the portraits.
    H065 · human LookInspect the named output against this requirement and its linked references.

    H064 · human LookInspect the named output against this requirement and its linked references.

  • No flat, hard direct-flash look.
    H021 · human LookInspect the named output against this requirement and its linked references.
    The portrait on “Summit speaker card - Marguerite Halloran” (speaker-card-r002.png) shows no hard direct-flash look: no hard flash shadow behind the head and no flat shine across the face.
